- plan-002-olmo2-s0/s1: DONE/success ✓

### 4. 📋 待办
- 诊断 Worker 回调 → 根据结果：(a) 若 r2 在跑且正常 → 更新 r2 状态为 RUNNING，等其完成；(b) 若 r2 也死了 → 更新所有状态为 DONE/failed，汇报 Director 决定是否第 4 次提交
- qwen06b 进程确认死亡 → 更新 registry 为 DONE/failed，检查已产出数据量，决定是否 resume
- 所有 Qwen 生成完成 → plan-002 转 DONE，合并 shard，开始 plan-003（cross_family_reference_model）

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
- **核心结论**：MVP macro-F1=0.84 验证 Claim 1（单生成器）；plan-001-fix 验证 Claim 2（Feature Complementarity，dual dynamics）
- **当前瓶颈**：plan-002（跨生成器）Pythia-1B 和 OLMo-2 已成功，Qwen3-1.7B 反复被 SIGHUP（操作层面问题非代码问题），Qwen3-0.6B 状态不明

- **Venue 时间线**：AAAI 2027 (Aug 2) primary / EMNLP ARR (May 25) stretch

Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps.       "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"experiments\": {\"plan-004\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"retry_count\": 1, \"created_at\": \"2026-05-15T00:22:57.901Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-15T01:02:50.582Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"Downstream utility: depth-stratified 数据对 OLMo-2-0425-1B CLM fine-tuning 的影响——depth0_only vs depth01 vs all vs size_matched vs binary_filtered，5 variants × 3 seeds\", \"notes\": \"FAILED: 根因是 OOM on GPU0（与 Qwen3-8B 共享 16.5GB），非单纯磁盘满。日志有 5 个 OOM Traceback（尝试分配 784MiB 但 GPU 仅剩 307MB）。磁盘满是次因（旧 checkpoints 22GB 占满根分区）。已由 plan-004-v2 在 cuda:1/2/3 重跑。\", \"completed_at\": \"2026-05-15T00:44:49.276Z\", \"extra\": {\"previous_failure\": {\"conclusion\": \"failed\", \"notes\": \"2026-05-15 08:41: Disk crisis resolved (root was 100%, now 25%). Symlinked experiments/downstream → /root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-fingerprint/experiments/downstream/. Killed old stalled PIDs. I'm seeing a GPU conflict between plan-004-v2 and plan-015-v2 both using cuda:2, but the timestamps suggest plan-004-v2 was restarted after the disk crisis around 08:41 UTC. Looking at the cron reconcile output, plan-014 and plan-004-v2 have active PIDs, but plan-015-v2 has no PID recorded despite being created at 00:56 UTC—it's likely the bootstrap CI analysis script completed quickly without proper status tracking. already completed but the registry wasn't updated, or it was never properly launched.

I should spawn a worker to check plan-015-v2 status and report to Director with the current comprehensive status. ```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50
